To address these challenges, we propose PixVL, a self-supervised post-training framework that introduces a unified Mask--Text Consistency Cycle, enabling pixel-level MLLMs to generate and self-verify regional descriptions and learn from unlabeled data. We found that direct cycle based solely on geometric reconstruction is unreliable because re-segmentation IoU does not faithfully reflect the semantic quality and referring sufficiency. PixVL therefore introduces confuser-aware semantic verification, which uses the model's confidence when it correctly chooses the target among highly similar candidate regions, and assigns zero reward to an incorrect choice. Meanwhile, PixVL performs cross-view verification using temporally separated video frames or geometrically transformed image views, preventing cyclic learning from collapsing to positional and shape shortcuts. Finally, a quality-coupled bidirectional learning strategy uses the highest-reward description to guide Text-to-Mask learning. This strategy transforms Region Understanding and Region Segmentation from competing tasks into mutual generators and verifiers. Experiments demonstrate that PixVL improves both region understanding task and segmentation task.

</details>


### [139] [ET-Prune: Evidence-Aware Dynamic Budgeting for Visual Token Pruning in Text-Rich MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01979)
*Zizhong Ding,Junxian Li,Kai Liu,Shaoqiu Zhang,Xiao Xiao,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ET-Prune是一个无需训练的视觉token剪枝框架，针对OCR等文本密集型多模态推理场景，将剪枝建模为证据分配问题，通过问题条件化的证据提取、文本区域保护和样本自适应的token预算动态保留关键证据，在约半数token下达到或超越全量基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法使用固定的token比例，但文本密集型输入（如OCR任务）中，决定性证据可能是少量数字、标签或字段，其相关性由问题指定。无差别剪枝会消除这些关键证据，同时保留视觉显著但不相关的区域，导致性能退化。

Method: ET-Prune包含三个核心组件：(1) 从解码器侧的部分query-key块推导问题条件化的证据图；(2) 保护文本类空间区域；(3) 将证据不确定性和密度转化为样本特定的token下限。通过三层渐进式中间层事件将序列推向该预算，对分散或文本密集的证据保留更多token，对集中证据更激进地剪枝。整个框架仅需每个配置一次的确定性推理，无需训练。

Result: ET-Prune在六个主干网络-基准测试组合中，以约一半的token数量在所有剪枝方法中领先或持平。在OCRBench-v2上，分别以1.80和0.68个百分点领先最强剪枝基线（Qwen3-VL-8B和InternVL3.5-8B）；在MMBench v1.1上达到0.8467的圆形精确匹配准确率（Vanilla为0.8437），平均保留54.45%的视觉token。

Conclusion: 证据感知的动态token预算在文本密集型多模态推理中展现出有利的观测质量-成本权衡，证明了无需训练即可通过证据分配策略实现高效且准确的视觉token剪枝。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference cost of multimodal large language models, but a fixed token ratio is poorly matched to text-rich inputs. In OCR-centric tasks, decisive evidence can be a small number, label, or field whose relevance is specified by the question; indiscriminate pruning can erase that evidence while retaining visually salient but irrelevant regions. We present ET-Prune, a training-free framework that casts pruning as evidence allocation. It derives question-conditioned evidence from a decoder-side partial query-key block, safeguards text-like spatial regions, and converts evidence uncertainty and density into a sample-specific token floor. Three progressive middle-layer events then move the sequence toward this budget, retaining more tokens for diffuse or text-dense evidence and pruning concentrated evidence more aggressively. At the observed point estimates from one deterministic pass per configuration, ET-Prune leads or ties among pruned methods in all six backbone-benchmark comparisons at roughly half tokens. On OCRBench-v2, it leads the strongest pruned baselines by 1.80 and 0.68 percentage points on Qwen3-VL-8B and InternVL3.5-8B, respectively, while retaining about half of the visual tokens; on MMBench v1.1, it reaches 0.8467 circular exact-matching accuracy versus 0.8437 for Vanilla at 54.45% average visual-token retention. These results show a favorable observed quality-cost trade-off for evidence-aware dynamic budgeting in text-rich multimodal inference.

</details>


### [140] [CORTIVA: Candidate-Score Fusion of Complementary Visual Teachers for EEG- and MEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01355)
*Junhan Wang,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CORTIVA，一种候选分数融合框架，用于从非侵入性脑活动（EEG/MEG）进行神经图像检索。它通过三条解码路径分别对齐异构视觉目标，独立对候选图像打分，再融合温度缩放后的分数向量进行排序，避免早期嵌入合并导致的信息丢失。在THINGS-EEG2基准上达到73.5% Top-1和95.3% Top-5，显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于EEG/MEG的零样本图像检索系统通常将异构视觉监督collapse为单一嵌入再进行排序，这种早期整合强制所有候选顺序共享同一相似性几何，丢失了编码器间的互补性分歧信息，限制了检索性能。

Method: CORTIVA框架包含三条解码路线，分别对齐到异构视觉目标；每条路线独立对同一候选库打分，最终仅融合温度缩放的分数向量再进行排名。通过路移除重训练和多种权重控制实验验证融合增益的稳健性，并使用DINOv2分析验证局部错误邻域和神经-视觉对应关系。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG2基准上，CORTIVA达到73.5% Top-1和95.3% Top-5（10名参与者），超过最强基线10.3和5.4个百分点；在THINGS-MEG上使用模态专属编码器达到42.4% Top-1。均匀权重下性能仍然保持，不依赖专门加权规则。

Conclusion: 候选分数融合被证明是一种简单且可验证的替代嵌入级整合的方案，能够有效保留多编码器互补信息，为神经图像检索提供新的方向。

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from non-invasive brain activity is central to neuroscience and brain-computer interfaces. Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) offers fine spatial detail, but its slow hemodynamics and burdensome acquisition limit temporally resolved decoding. Electroencephalography (EEG) and magnetoencephalography (MEG) provide millisecond resolution, making image retrieval compelling: identify the viewed image from one neural response and a fixed candidate bank. Contrastive alignment to pretrained visual representations enables zero-shot retrieval from EEG and MEG, but most systems collapse heterogeneous visual supervision into a single embedding before ranking. This early consolidation imposes one similarity geometry on every candidate order and removes encoder-specific disagreements from the final ranking. We propose CORTIVA, a candidate-score fusion framework that preserves this complementary evidence. Three decoding routes are aligned to heterogeneous visual targets, score the same indexed candidates independently, and combine only their temperature-scaled score vectors before ranking. On the 200-way THINGS-EEG2 benchmark, CORTIVA reaches 73.5% Top-1 and 95.3% Top-5 across ten participants, exceeding the strongest reported baseline by 10.3 and 5.4 percentage points. With a modality-specific neural encoder, the same fusion principle reaches 42.4% Top-1 on THINGS-MEG. Matched route-removal retraining and four weight controls demonstrate that CORTIVA's gain arises from integrating complementary route scores and persists with uniform weighting, without requiring a specialized weighting rule. Independent DINOv2 analyses further reproduce the local error neighborhoods and posterior neural-visual correspondence. These results establish candidate-score fusion as a simple and testable alternative to embedding-level consolidation for neural image retrieval.

</details>


### [141] [HAFI-VLM: A Frequency Perspective for Diagnosing and Enhancing Visual Perception in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02124)
*Jin Cui,Chuanchang Su,Jiayi Lu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HAFI-VLM，通过任务条件化的频率增强路径（层级自适应频率注入）改善视觉语言模型在细粒度视觉证据上的可靠性，在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在需要细粒度视觉证据的预测中仍然不可靠，其根源在于预训练视觉编码器存在"频谱响应刚性"问题——尽管不同图像和任务存在显著的频率差异，编码器却保持固定且几乎不变的层级频谱特征，且由于编码器只接收图像输入，无法根据当前查询需求调整频谱提取方式。

Method: 提出HAFI-VLM架构，包含两个核心组件：(1) 层级自适应频率注入（HAFI），在不同编码器深度使用文本调制、空间对齐的交叉注意力检索互补的低/中/高频证据；(2) 视觉增强层适配器，重新校准浅层LLM注意力以有效利用增强后的视觉标记。整体在保留预训练语义表示的同时引入任务条件化的频率通路。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL上的实验表明，该方法在通用VQA、文本密集理解和幻觉鲁棒性方面都取得一致提升，优于表示级增强方法及大多数基于分辨率或裁剪的方法，且无需额外的高分辨率编码。机制分析证实HAFI恢复了任务依赖的频谱分配并保留语义注意力。

Conclusion: 频率增强是改善VLM视觉感知的一条独立且有效的技术路线，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable when predictions require fine-grained visual evidence. We identify a previously overlooked cause: spectral response rigidity. Despite substantial frequency variation across images and tasks, pretrained vision encoders exhibit persistent, encoder-specific layerwise spectral profiles that change only marginally under downstream fine-tuning. Since pretrained vision encoders only receive images, they cannot adapt spectral extraction to the evidence required by the current query. We therefore propose HAFI-VLM, which introduces a task-conditioned frequency pathway while preserving the pretrained semantic representation. Hierarchical Adaptive Frequency Injection (HAFI) retrieves complementary low-, mid-, and high-frequency evidence at multiple encoder depths using text-modulated, spatially aligned cross-attention. A Visual Enrichment Layer Adapter further recalibrates shallow LLM attention to effectively utilize the enriched visual tokens. Experiments on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL demonstrate consistent improvements in general VQA, text-rich understanding, and hallucination robustness, outperforming representation-level enhancement methods and most resolution- or cropping-based approaches without additional high-resolution encoding. Mechanistic analyses show that HAFI restores task-dependent spectral allocation while retaining semantic attention, establishing frequency enrichment as a distinct and effective route for improving VLM perception.

</details>


### [142] [Harnessing Adversarial Distillation to Customise Debiased, Disease-Specific Pathology Foundation Models for Breast Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01356)
*Zhiwei Chen,Yang Hu,Yuxiang Xiao,Yakun Ju,Tianyang Zhang,Yingxue Xu,Wei Li,Hao Chen,Jens Rittscher,Kaixiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartStu是一个通过对抗蒸馏将多个通用病理基础模型（PFM）的知识压缩到轻量级乳腺癌专用模型中，同时缓解预训练带来的混杂偏差的框架。该模型比通用PFM小30倍以上，在下游任务上性能基本持平甚至略有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用病理基础模型参数量高达数十亿导致计算成本高昂，且泛癌多中心预训练引入分布偏移和非生物偏差（如位点特异特征、疾病患病率不平衡），促使模型依赖捷径学习而忽视特定癌症类型所需的细微形态特征，限制了其在特定疾病场景中的部署。

Method: SmartStu包含三个核心组件：1）多教师集成蒸馏，将多个教师PFM的表示蒸馏到轻量级学生骨干网络中；2）对抗蒸馏，训练一个专用的噪声模型来预测蒸馏数据上的干扰性边缘线索，并将其作为反例，通过对抗目标鼓励学生识别但抑制这些对干扰目标具有预测性的特征；3）辅助自监督目标结合伪影注入，进一步增强学生模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个外部队列（Yale HER2、SLN-Breast和BRACS）上使用多种小型骨干网络进行验证，SmartStu生成的乳腺癌专用PFM比通用PFM小30倍以上，同时在下游性能（bAcc和AUC）上基本保持甚至有时有所提升。

Conclusion: SmartStu证明了通过对抗蒸馏可以有效定制紧凑、疾病特异性的基础模型，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，并通过识别和抑制干扰特征来缓解预训练带来的混杂偏差。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) provide strong tissue representations and have become central to digital pathology. However, deployment in disease-specific settings is limited by 1) the high computational cost of billion-parameter PFMs and 2) distribution mismatch and non-biological bias inherited from pan-cancer, multi-centre pre-training, including site-specific signatures and imbalanced disease prevalence. These factors can encourage shortcut learning and under-emphasise subtle morphology required for reliable modelling of a specific cancer type. We present SmartStu (a Smart Student), a framework to customise compact, breast-cancer-specific PFMs via distillation whilst mitigating confounding. SmartStu distils representations from multiple teacher PFMs into a lightweight student backbone. Crucially, we introduce adversarial distillation that leverages a dedicated noise model trained to predict nuisance, edge-dominated cues on the distillation set. Using this noise model as a counterexample, the adversarial objective encourages the student to recognise, yet suppress, features predictive of nuisance targets. We further incorporate multi-teacher ensemble distillation and an auxiliary self-supervised objective with artefact injection. We validate SmartStu on three external cohorts (Yale HER2, SLN-Breast, and BRACS) with multiple tiny backbones. SmartStu yields breast-cancer-specific PFMs that are over $30\times$ smaller than general PFMs whilst largely preserving, and sometimes improving, downstream performance measured by balanced accuracy (bAcc) and AUC. Code is available at https://github.com/zwchen03/advDistall.

</details>


### [143] [UEmbed: Unified Sparse and Dense Multimodal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02583)
*Tingyu Song,Mingxin Li,Yanzhao Zhang,Dingkun Long,Pengjun Xie,Zhijie Nie,Yilun Zhao,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UEmbed，一个仅解码器的多模态嵌入模型，通过一次因果前向传播同时生成稀疏词法表示和稠密表示，将稀疏检索从单模态扩展到多模态，并在2B、4B、9B三个规模上发布模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型稀疏检索（LSR）仍受限于编码器式双向架构，且扩展到多模态时依赖辅助的跨模态模块。作者希望突破这些限制，实现一个统一的、无需额外模块的多模态稀疏检索范式。

Method: UEmbed采用仅解码器架构，在输入中附加N个可学习的特殊token，并将词表划分为N个不相交子集。每个特殊token的因果隐藏状态预测其分配子集上的稀疏权重，所有子集拼接形成完整的稀疏向量，同时在同一前向过程中生成稠密表示。

Result: UEmbed-9B在MMEB-v2上达到71.8（稠密）和71.0（稀疏），优于使用公开数据训练的多模态嵌入模型（如RzenEmbed）；在BEIR上与强稠密和稀疏基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: UEmbed提供了新范式：在一个模型中统一稠密和稀疏嵌入，同时将稀疏检索扩展至统一的文本和多模态输入，并在有效性、效率和智能体应用三个维度上展示了实用价值。

Abstract: Sparse retrieval underpins modern search systems, from web search to retrieval-augmented generation. Existing work has introduced Learned Sparse Retrieval (LSR) to push beyond exact lexical matching toward richer semantics. Yet LSR has so far remained tied to encoder-style bidirectional architectures, and its extension to multimodal settings still relies heavily on auxiliary cross-modal modules. To address these limitations, we introduce UEmbed (Unified Embedding), a decoder-only multimodal embedding model that produces both sparse lexical and dense representations in one causal forward pass. UEmbed appends N learnable special tokens to the input and partitions the vocabulary into N disjoint subsets. Each token's causal hidden state predicts sparse weights over its assigned subset, and the N subsets are concatenated into the full sparse vector. Trained on public data, we release UEmbed at 2B, 4B, and 9B scales. UEmbed-9B reaches 71.8 (dense) and 71.0 (sparse) on MMEB-v2, outperforming multimodal embedding models trained on publicly available data (e.g., RzenEmbed). On BEIR, UEmbed also remains competitive with strong dense and sparse baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical utility of UEmbed across three dimensions: effectiveness, efficiency, and agentic applications. Overall, UEmbed offers a new paradigm: it unifies dense and sparse embeddings in one model, while further extending sparse retrieval to unify text and multimodal inputs.

</details>


### [144] [Understanding Synergistic Interactions among Pathology Foundation Models via Adaptive Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01370)
*Yuxiang Xiao,Yang Hu,Bin Li,Tianyang Zhang,Zexi Li,Huazhu Fu,Jens Rittscher,Kaixiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaFusion是一个轻量级自适应融合框架，通过低维特征压缩和样本条件门控模块整合多个冻结的病理学基础模型（PFMs）的互补信号，在提升预测精度的同时提供基于贡献度的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学基础模型（PFMs）通过自监督预训练在病理图像上提供强大的tile级表示，但由于数据、架构和目标函数选择的多样性和不透明性，导致潜在的表征偏差，限制了鲁棒性并模糊了各模型的专长方向。需要一种方法整合多个PFMs的互补信号。

Method: AdaFusion框架包含两个核心组件：(1)低维特征压缩，降低特征维度；(2)样本条件门控模块，能够按模型维度（可选地按通道维度）重新加权不同PFM的贡献，实现自适应的特征融合。

Result: 在三个公开基准上（治疗反应预测、前列腺癌分级和空间基因表达推断）验证，AdaFusion一致优于单个PFMs和其他融合基线方法，并提供了与模型偏好和形态学模式一致的可解释组织可视化。

Conclusion: AdaFusion不仅提升了多PFM融合的预测精度，还通过贡献驱动的解释机制揭示了模型特定的偏好和跨组织表型的协同交互，为病理学基础模型的集成应用提供了一种轻量且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) provide strong tile-level representations via self-supervised pre-training on large-scale pathology images. Yet, PFMs are developed under diverse and often opaque data, architecture, and objective choices, inducing latent representational biases that limit robustness and obscure what each model specialises in. We present AdaFusion, a lightweight adaptive fusion framework that integrates complementary signals from multiple frozen PFMs through (1) low-dimensional feature compression and (2) a sample-conditioned gating module that reweights model-wise (and optionally channel-wise) contributions. Beyond improving predictive accuracy, AdaFusion provides contribution-driven interpretation that offers evidence consistent with model-specific preferences and synergistic interactions across tissue phenotypes. We evaluate AdaFusion on three public benchmarks spanning treatment response prediction, prostate cancer grading, and spatial gene expression inference. AdaFusion consistently outperforms individual PFMs and other fusion baselines, while providing interpretable tissue visualisation which aligns model preferences with morphological patterns. Code is available at: https://github.com/xyx-98/PathoOracle.

</details>


### [145] [FineMoLA: Towards Fine-Grained Motion-Language Alignment from Clip-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01392)
*Tongyan Wang,Zhengyuan Li,Muhan Lin,Shengyang Luo,Yifan Shen,Aniket Bera,Baijian Yang,Yingjie Victor Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FineMoLA，一个弱监督框架，通过最优传输问题从片段级标注中学习细粒度的帧-短语对应关系，用于文本驱动的动作生成和动作-文本对齐，无需人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本条件动作生成模型虽然有大规模动作-语言数据集，但监督仅停留在片段级别，缺少帧与语言之间的显式时间对应，这限制了细粒度的动作-文本对齐和时间精确的生成。

Method: FineMoLA首先将长描述分割为携带动作信息的短语，然后将动作-语言对齐建模为最优传输问题，利用熵正则化和Sinkhorn迭代在全局约束下高效推断伪帧级对齐，实现帧与短语之间的多对多匹配。

Result: 在SnapMoGen数据集上的实验表明，FineMoLA学习到的对齐在动作-文本接地任务上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: FineMoLA通过最优传输框架，仅依赖片段级弱监督即可获得细粒度的帧与短语对齐，提升了动作-文本接地能力，为时间精确的动作生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Text-conditioned human motion generation has made rapid progress with the emergence of large-scale motion--language datasets. However, even datasets with rich long-form descriptions typically provide supervision only at the clip level, without explicit temporal correspondence between motion frames and language. This limits fine-grained motion--text grounding and temporally precise generation. We propose FineMoLA, a weakly supervised framework that learns fine-grained frame--phrase correspondence directly from clip-level annotations. Our method first segments long-form descriptions into action-bearing phrases, and then formulates motion--language alignment as an optimal transport problem, which naturally models many-to-many relations between motion frames and text under global constraints. With entropic regularization and Sinkhorn iterations, FineMoLA efficiently infers pseudo frame-level alignments without human labeling. Experiments on SnapMoGen demonstrate that the learned alignments outperform baselines in motion--text grounding.

</details>


### [146] [Training-Free Out-of-Distribution Detection for Pathology Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01407)
*Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Jerry Jierui Lou,Akanimoh Adeleye,Haci Ali Kahya,Jana Lipkova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ZIO，一种无需训练的、多模态OOD检测器，用于病理学全切片图像（WSIs），利用视觉-语言病理学基础模型构建文本和视觉原型，通过原型收缩机制整合信息，在超过14,700张WSIs的五个独立数据集上超越了40种SOTA OOD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI方法在医学中的安全部署需要可靠的防护措施，以检测输入数据是否偏离训练分布，确保模型只在其专业范围内提供预测。然而，OOD检测在计算病理学领域发展不足，因为千兆像素WSIs、疾病亚型间的细微差异以及组织制备的变异性给传统OOD方法带来了独特挑战。

Method: ZIO是一种训练-free的多模态OOD检测器，针对病理学WSIs设计。它利用视觉-语言病理学基础模型，构建分布内类别的文本和视觉原型，并通过原型收缩机制整合两者的互补信息来推导OOD分数。ZIO同时支持幻灯片级和补丁级基础模型的公式化。

Result: 在超过14,700张WSIs的五个独立联盟数据集上进行了广泛评估，涵盖罕见疾病和近OOD设置等多样化的临床相关领域偏移。ZIO在性能上持续优于单模态原型和40种SOTA OOD方法。

Conclusion: ZIO展示了多模态表示在OOD检测中的优势，为AI在临床实践中的更安全部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: Safe deployment of AI methods in medicine requires robust guardrails that detect when input data deviate from the training distribution to ensure that models provide predictions only within their scope of expertise and abstain otherwise. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection can provide such safeguards and is extensively studied in general computer vision. Yet, it remains underdeveloped in computational pathology, where gigapixel whole-slide images (WSIs), subtle differences between disease subtypes, and variability in tissue preparation pose unique challenges for conventional OOD methods. We propose ZIO, a training-free, multimodal OOD detector for pathology WSIs that leverages vision--language pathology foundation models (FMs). ZIO constructs text and visual prototypes of in-distribution classes and integrates their complementary information through a prototype shrinkage mechanism to derive OOD scores. We provide the ZIO formulation for both slide- and patch-level FMs. We evaluate ZIO across diverse clinically relevant domain shifts, including rare diseases and near-OOD settings. Extensive evaluation of over 14,700 WSIs from five independent consortia shows that ZIO consistently outperforms both unimodal prototypes and 40 state-of-the-art OOD methods. These results demonstrate the benefits of multimodal representation for OOD detection and pave the way towards safer AI deployment in clinical practice.

</details>


### [147] [PackingGPT: 3D Packing Agent for Real Furniture in Last-Mile Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01427)
*Yi You,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文针对真实家具装载场景中的三维装箱问题，提出了一个异构家具入车装箱的基准数据集和PackingGPT基线模型，并验证了质心（CoM）约束对装载稳定性的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准集装箱装载算法忽视了真实场景中的复杂条件，如异构箱子（不同尺寸和重量）以及容器内已有物品（如杂货）的存在。将家具装载到个人车辆中正是这类被忽视的复杂任务，需要物理稳定放置。

Method: 1) 构建基于真实家具公司平板包装数据的基准数据集，通过家族级外推生成多样化的长宽高和重量数据；2) 提出PackingGPT框架，借鉴乐高组装过程，将异构箱子（砖块）逐步放入不规则的剩余货物空间（作品）中，实现顺序放置。

Result: 在不考虑质心约束的情况下，五种基线装箱方法在轿车模拟中平均有10-40%的箱子未能通过稳定性检查。而在放置过程中强制执行质心约束训练LLP模型后，SUV-500场景的失败率降至0.67%。

Conclusion: 质心约束对装箱稳定性至关重要，PackingGPT框架结合质心约束能够显著降低装载失败率，为异构家具入车装箱任务提供了有效的基线解决方案。

Abstract: 3D bin packing rectangular items into standardised containers to maximise space utilisation under geometric shipping automation. Loading a furniture purchase into a personal vehicle is the same task, but under more complex conditions that standard container loading algorithms ignore. This paper addresses the physically stable placement under these realistic conditions with heterogeneous boxes (e.g. varying dimensions and weights) and occupied containers (e.g. groceries). This paper provides a real-world benchmark dataset and baseline model for the Heterogeneous furniture-in-vehicle packing task. The dataset uses real furniture company flat-pack packaging data covering a large number of catalogue products via family-level extrapolation with diversity length, widths, heights, and weights. We also propose a PackingGPT framework for packing as a sequential placement inspired by the Lego assembly process, where heterogeneous boxes of varying dimensions (bricks) are placed step-by-step into the irregular remaining cargo space (creations). Five baseline packing methods were tested on our dataset without considering the Centre-of- Mass (CoM) constraints. In sedan car simulations, 10-40% of placed boxes failed the stability check on average. When the LLP model was trained on packing sequences with CoM constraints enforced during placement, the failure rate dropped to 0.67% (SUV-500).

</details>


### [148] [VGER: Voxel-Guided Global Event Ranking for Event Cloud Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01470)
*Youxin Jiang,Baoheng Fu,Hongwei Ren,Xiangqian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VGER（Voxel-Guided Global Event Ranking），一个针对基于点的事件流网络的免训练归因框架。VGER结合事件级梯度证据与任务感知的体素扰动证据，将区域贡献转化为事件级归因分数，并引入统一的事件排序策略。在三个基准、九种数据集-骨干网络组合上，VGER一致优于点级显著性基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机产生稀疏、异步的事件流，具有丰富的时空信息。虽然基于事件的模型已展现出强性能，但识别模型预测背后的事件级证据对于提升模型透明度和可靠性至关重要。现有方法直接套用点云的点级显著性方法，虽能提供细粒度归因，却忽略了事件特有的时空结构。

Method: VGER是一个免训练归因框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 结合事件级梯度证据；(2) 引入任务感知的体素扰动证据，将区域贡献转换为事件级归因分数，同时保留细粒度分辨率；(3) 提出统一的事件排序策略，高排名事件被视为预测关键事件，低排名事件被认为对预测影响有限。

Result: 在三个基于事件的基准上，使用PointNet、PointNet++和EventMamba三种骨干网络进行评估。在九种数据集-骨干网络组合设置中，VGER在高尾和低尾删除性能上均一致优于点级显著性基线方法。

Conclusion: VGER通过结合事件级梯度证据与体素扰动证据，有效弥补了点级显著性方法在事件时空结构建模上的不足，为基于点的事件云网络提供了更可靠、更透明的归因解释。

Abstract: Event cameras produce sparse and asynchronous event streams that provide rich spatio-temporal information for efficient perception. Recent advances in event-based models have demonstrated strong performance by directly modeling asynchronous events without dense frame reconstruction. However, identifying the event-level evidence behind their predictions is crucial for improving model transparency and reliability. Directly adapting point-level saliency methods from point clouds provides fine-grained attribution but overlooks event-specific spatio-temporal structures. To address this limitation, we propose Voxel-Guided Global Event Ranking (VGER), a training-free attribution framework for point-based event cloud networks. VGER combines event-level gradient evidence with task-aware voxel perturbation evidence, transferring regional contribution into event-level attribution scores while preserving fine-grained resolution. Furthermore, VGER introduces a unified event ranking strategy, where high-ranked events are expected to be prediction-critical and low-ranked events are expected to have limited influence on predictions. We evaluate VGER on three event-based benchmarks with PointNet, PointNet++, and EventMamba. Across nine dataset-backbone settings, VGER consistently improves both high-tail and low-tail deletion performance over point-level saliency baselines.

</details>


### [149] [Towards Compact Unified Multimodal Tracking: Synergizing Knowledge Distillation with Structural Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01488)
*Yuqi Li,Yuedong Tan,Huiran Duan,Weilun Feng,Chuanguang Yang,Zhulin An,Zongwei Wu,Shiping Wen,Tingwen Huang,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多模态目标跟踪模型的双对齐蒸馏框架（Dual-Alignment Distillation），通过解耦知识转移为空间表征对齐和语义分布对齐两个互补流，在压缩预测头解码器架构的同时弥补轻量学生模型与重型教师模型之间的能力差距，实现了5倍加速且保持优异精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态目标跟踪虽凭借多传感器互补数据取得了显著鲁棒性，但最先进模型的计算负担过重，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上。现有工作大多关注主干网络压缩，忽视了预测头这一关键效率瓶颈。

Method: （1）识别预测头（解码器）为效率瓶颈并精简其架构；（2）系统分析17种蒸馏策略；（3）提出双对齐蒸馏框架，将知识转移解耦为两个互补流：空间表征对齐（特征蒸馏，增强学生模型对前景目标的空间关注，解决"在哪里跟踪"）和语义分布对齐（logit蒸馏，对齐决策边界并传递判别性暗知识，解决"跟踪什么"）。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于复杂的现有最先进方法。蒸馏模型在RGBT234上达到91.5%的MPR，在单张RTX 4090上运行速度达54 FPS，相比教师模型实现5倍加速，同时保持更高精度。

Conclusion: 通过将知识蒸馏解耦为空间与语义两个对齐流，可以有效解决轻量化模型压缩带来的能力差距问题，实现多模态目标跟踪模型在边缘设备上的高效实时部署，兼顾速度与精度。

Abstract: Unified multimodal object tracking has achieved remarkable robustness by leveraging complementary sensor data (e.g., RGB, Thermal, Depth), yet the heavy computational burden of state-of-the-art models hinders their deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. In this work, we identify the prediction head as a critical but often overlooked efficiency bottleneck. By strategically streamlining the decoder architecture, we unlock the potential for real-time inference but simultaneously introduce a capacity gap between the lightweight student and the heavy teacher. To resolve this, we conduct a systematic analysis of 17 distillation strategies and introduce a Dual-Alignment Distillation framework. Our key insight is that effective compression requires decoupling knowledge transfer into two complementary streams: (1) Spatial Representation Alignment, which employs feature distillation to sharpen the student's spatial focus on foreground targets ("Where to track"); and (2) Semantic Distribution Alignment, which utilizes logit-based distillation to align decision boundaries and transfer discriminative dark knowledge ("What to track"). Extensive experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms complex state-of-the-art methods. Notably, our distilled model achieves 91.5% MPR on RGBT234 and operates at 54 FPS on a single RTX 4090, representing a 5x speedup over the teacher model while maintaining superior accuracy.

</details>


### [150] [GaussianSelector: Lightweight Human-Guided Object Selection in 3D Gaussian Splatting with Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01492)
*Baihan Yang,Tiexin Li,Yuheng Liu,Xin Lin,Xinke Li,Xiaohui Xie,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GaussianSelector，一种无需训练的交互式3D物体选择框架，直接从稀疏视图和稀疏涂鸦引导中操作原生高斯原语，通过构造连续加权图并求解全局图割能量最小化来实现完整的3D物体选择，同时支持多轮交互精化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-based方法要么重新训练高斯表示以嵌入逐对象标签，要么构建密集多视角SAM观测，都需要大量计算和密集视角覆盖，这在实践中很少可用。因此需要一种轻量级的、适用于稀疏视图场景的交互式3D物体选择方法。

Method: 1) 将密集高斯粗糙化为几何一致的超点；2) 使用外观和空间线索构建连续性加权图；3) 通过可见性感知透射覆盖率将稀疏用户涂鸦提升到3D；4) 将选择问题建模为全局图割能量最小化，将稀疏证据传播到完整3D物体；5) 支持多轮精化，用户可从额外视角迭代纠正选择。

Result: 实验表明GaussianSelector在竞争质量上达到与最先进的多视图SAM方法相当的水平，同时显著减少了交互视图数量并大幅降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: GaussianSelector凭借其轻量级、无需训练和低计算开销的特性，非常适合人机交互式3D场景编辑和现实部署场景中的3D资产提取。

Abstract: Selecting a complete 3D object from a reconstructed scene with minimal user effort is essential for practical scene editing and embodied interaction. Existing 3DGS-based methods either retrain the Gaussian representation to embed per-object labels, or build dense multi-view SAM observations, both requiring heavy computation and dense viewpoint coverage that is rarely available in practice. We present GaussianSelector, a training-free framework for interactive 3D object selection from sparse views and sparse scribble guidance. Operating directly on native Gaussian primitives, we coarsen dense Gaussians into geometrically coherent superpoints and construct a continuity-weighted graph using appearance and spatial cues. Sparse user scribbles are lifted into 3D via visibility-aware transmittance coverage, and selection is solved as a global graph-cut energy minimization that propagates sparse evidence to a complete 3D object. This design naturally supports multi-round refinement, where users iteratively correct the selection from additional viewpoints to progressively improve the result. Experiments demonstrate that GaussianSelector achieves competitive selection quality against state-of-the-art multi-view SAM-based methods, while requiring significantly fewer interaction views and substantially lower computational overhead. These properties make it well suited for human-in-the-loop 3D scene editing and 3D asset extraction in real-world deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [151] [Probing the 3D Object-Level Understanding of Pre-Trained Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01495)
*Robin Kim,Colin Samplawski,Benjamin M. Marlin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练的2D检测变压器（如DETR系列）中物体级嵌入是否隐含了物体的3D属性信息（如深度和相对于相机的3D位置），通过线性和非线性探针发现这些模型在完全没有3D监督的情况下，能够从中恢复出惊人的3D空间信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DETR等检测变压器在2D检测任务上表现出色，但人们对这种纯2D预训练模型是否隐式编码了3D空间信息知之甚少。理解这一点有助于挖掘现有2D模型在3D感知任务上的潜力。

Method: 使用线性和非线性探针（probing）方法，从预训练的2D检测变压器（如DETR及其扩展）的物体级嵌入中，探测物体的深度信息和相对相机的3D位置信息，在多种检测变压器模型上进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，尽管模型在预训练期间完全没有接触3D监督信号，这些2D DETR模型的物体级嵌入中惊人地包含了丰富且有价值的3D物体属性信息，这种能力在此前是未知的。

Conclusion: 2D检测变压器在纯2D训练下隐式学习到了3D空间理解能力，这为将2D预训练模型迁移或扩展至3D感知任务提供了新的可能性和理论依据。

Abstract: Detection transformer models, including DETR and its extensions, learn to output a set of object-level embeddings that can be simultaneously decoded into 2D bounding boxes and class distributions. In this paper, we investigate what pre-trained 2D detection transformers understand about the 3D properties of objects. Specifically, we investigate the extent to which properties including the depth of objects from the camera and the 3D location of objects relative to the camera can be recovered from object-level embeddings using linear and non-linear probes. Across a range of detection transformer models, our results show a surprisingly strong and previously unknown ability of 2D DETR models to represent useful information about the 3D properties of objects, despite the complete lack of 3D supervision during model pre-training.

</details>


### [152] [Rolling Shutter Camera Self-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01509)
*Yongcong Zhang,Navid Rabbani,Bangyan Liao,Chengbo Wang,Yizhen Lao,Adrien Bartoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标定靶的卷帘快门(Rolling Shutter)相机自标定方法，通过联合两种互补的RS成像模型，在束调整中同时估计相机内参和读出时间比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RS相机标定方法依赖标定靶或专用硬件，限制了其在无约束场景中的应用，需要一种无需标定的RS相机自标定方法。

Method: 将两种互补的RS成像模型结合为统一的双投影模型：第一种将RS成像建模为逐行位姿表示的连续时间轨迹估计，第二种将RS图像视为时间畸变的全局快门图像并需估计校正场。在束调整中，每个3D点同时在行相关时间戳和参考时间戳上受连续轨迹约束。

Result: 通过大量仿真实验分析了多种实现在不同条件下的适用性，真实数据实验验证了方法的精度、鲁棒性和实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了无需标定靶的RS相机自标定，可直接从图像序列估计相机内参和读出时间比，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Rolling shutter (RS) cameras are widely used in consumer devices, but their row-wise exposure causes distortions under motion, making geometric 3D vision problems dependent on both camera intrinsics and readout time ratio. Existing RS calibration methods rely on calibration targets or specialised hardware, limiting their use in unconstrained settings. We present the first self-calibration method for RS cameras that directly estimates camera intrinsics and the readout time ratio from image sequences, without requiring calibration targets. The method is implemented as a self-calibrating bundle adjustment (BA), which critically depends on the RS imaging model. We combine two known complementary models. The first formulates RS imaging as continuous-time trajectory estimation under a row-wise pose representation. The second interprets RS images as temporally distorted global shutter (GS) images and requires to estimate correction fields. The combination is non-trivial and results in a unified dual-projection model, in which each 3D point is simultaneously constrained at both row-dependent and reference timestamps along a shared continuous trajectory, enforcing stronger geometric and temporal consistency. Extensive simulations analyse the applicability of several implementations under varying conditions, and real data experiments demonstrate the accuracy, robustness, and practical effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [153] [UCBound-Net: Uncertainty-Guided Boundary-Aware Continual Learning for Domain-Incremental Ultrasound Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01518)
*Mohammad Amanour Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UCBound-Net，一种利用MC Dropout不确定性作为空间遗忘风险代理的持续分割框架，通过不确定性加权边界蒸馏、不确定性校准正则化和不确定性引导的样本选择三个协同组件，在医学影像跨域持续学习中有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床影像持续学习面临双重挑战：模型需在获取新解剖领域知识的同时保留先前任务表征。现有缓解策略（如正则化和知识蒸馏）对所有空间区域一视同仁，忽略了预测不确定性与遗忘倾向之间的强相关性。

Method: 提出三个协同组件：(i)不确定性加权边界蒸馏，在高熵区域放大教师模型的知识迁移信号；(ii)不确定性校准正则化，显式惩罚过度自信的错误预测；(iii)不确定性引导的样本选择，优先存储边界区域预测熵最高的样本。

Result: 在乳腺超声(BUSI)到甲状腺超声(TN3K)的序列域增量基准上，UCBound-Net将BWT从-0.173改善至-0.098，两任务平均DSC达0.755，且无需任务边界监督，消融实验验证了各组件独立贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架为临床图像分割提供了一条实用的不确定性感知持续学习路径，各组件独立有效且协同作用，优于基线方法。

Abstract: Continual learning in clinical imaging faces a dual challenge: a model must assimilate knowledge from new anatomical domains while retaining representations learned from prior tasks, a problem known as catastrophic forgetting. Existing mitigation strategies, including regularization and knowledge distillation, treat all spatial regions equally, ignoring the fact that prediction uncertainty is strongly correlated with the propensity for forgetting. We introduce UCBound-Net, a continual segmentation framework that exploits Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout uncertainty as a spatial proxy for forgetting risk. Our method contributes three synergistic components: (i) uncertainty-weighted boundary distillation, which amplifies the knowledge transfer signal at high-entropy regions of the frozen teacher; (ii) uncertainty-calibration regularization, which explicitly penalizes overconfident erroneous predictions; and (iii) uncertainty-guided exemplar selection, a memory buffer that preferentially stores samples whose boundary regions exhibit the highest predictive entropy. Evaluated on a sequential domain-incremental benchmark comprising breast ultrasound (BUSI, Task 1) followed by thyroid ultrasound (TN3K, Task 2), UCBound-Net reduces forgetting relative to naive fine-tuning, achieving a backward transfer (BWT) of -0.098 compared with -0.173, while obtaining an average Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) of 0.755 across both tasks. The proposed framework outperforms baseline methods without requiring task-boundary supervision. An ablation study further demonstrates that each component contributes independently to forgetting mitigation, providing a practical pathway toward uncertainty-aware continual learning for clinical image segmentation.

</details>


### [154] [ST-LoRA: Single Trajectory LoRA Ensemble for Uncertainty Aware Agricultural Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01530)
*Mohamed Farag,Genc Hoxha,Yahia Maleki,Chris McCool,Ribana Roscher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-LoRA，一种结合低秩适配（LoRA）与快照集成的参数高效集成框架，仅使用不到10%的可训练参数构建多样化集成成员，在农业语义分割任务中匹配或超越全秩集成性能，同时显著降低训练时间、推理延迟和存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字农业中的可靠决策支持需要准确的预测和良好校准的不确定性估计，特别是在语义分割等密集预测任务中。集成方法虽能提供强不确定性量化，但其计算和内存需求限制了实际应用；而单模型近似往往在不确定性质量与效率之间进行妥协。现有方法难以同时实现高质量不确定性与参数效率。

Method: ST-LoRA从单一训练轨迹中构建多样化集成成员：每个成员共享冻结的预训练骨干网络，仅通过轻量级低秩适配器（LoRA）相互区分，结合快照集成策略在不同时间点保存模型状态形成集成。关键创新在于通过消融实验发现前馈层而非注意力层是密集预测任务中LoRA的关键目标，这与语言模型中仅关注注意力层的惯例相反。

Result: 在两个农业数据集（GrowliFlower-L和BUP20）上，使用SegFormer和Mask2Former架构评估，ST-LoRA在分割精度和校准方面匹配或超越全秩集成，同时在图像/像素级OoD检测、分布偏移下的校准稳定性和跨种子的方差方面始终匹配或优于Snapshot Ensemble、MC Dropout和Deep Deterministic Uncertainty等高效基线，且参数和计算量大幅减少。

Conclusion: LoRA高效的集成适配方法是构建不确定性感知农业视觉系统的极有效且实用的方案，为资源受限场景下的可靠决策支持提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Reliable decision-support in digital agriculture requires accurate predictions and well-calibrated uncertainty estimates, particularly for dense prediction tasks such as semantic segmentation. Ensemble methods provide strong uncertainty quantification, but their computational and memory demands limit practical use, while single-model approximations often trade off uncertainty quality for efficiency. We propose ST-LoRA, a parameter-efficient ensemble framework that builds diverse ensemble members from a single training trajectory by combining Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with snapshot ensembling. Each member shares a frozen pretrained backbone and differs only in lightweight low-rank adapters, reducing trainable parameters to under 10% of the full model while preserving ensemble diversity. We evaluate across two agricultural datasets - GrowliFlower-L (cauliflower, open field) and BUP20 (sweet pepper, glasshouse) - using SegFormer and Mask2Former, covering in-distribution performance, calibration under distribution shift, and out-of-distribution detection. Ablations show feed-forward layers, not attention layers, are the critical LoRA target for dense prediction, contrary to the attention-only convention from language models. ST-LoRA matches or exceeds full-rank ensembles in segmentation accuracy and calibration across both datasets and architectures, while substantially reducing training time, inference latency, memory footprint, and storage requirements. Against efficient baselines - Snapshot Ensemble, MC Dropout, and Deep Deterministic Uncertainty - ST-LoRA consistently matches or outperforms them in image/pixel-level OoD detection, calibration stability under shift, and cross-seed variance, with far fewer parameters and lower compute. These results show LoRA-efficient ensemble adaptation is a highly effective, practical approach for uncertainty-aware agricultural vision systems.

</details>


### [155] [Recursive Vision Language Models for General Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01534)
*Omid Nejati Manzari,Guillaume Lajoie,Hassan Rivaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R-Qwen，一个基于预训练Qwen架构的递归推理框架，通过程序性自递归和深度监督反复精炼候选解，结合递归模型的结构化迭代计算与预训练LLM的推理先验，在八个符号推理基准上优于现有递归模型和更大的LLM，并在ARC-AGI上取得27.6%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬符号推理任务（如数独、迷宫寻路、ARC）对LLM仍具挑战性，因为固定深度的自回归推理限制了系统性搜索、精炼和回溯能力。虽然递归模型（如HRM、TRM）通过迭代隐状态精炼解决了这一局限，但它们通常是任务特定的，且未利用预训练语言先验。

Method: R-Qwen基于预训练的Qwen骨干构建，通过程序性自递归和深度监督反复精炼候选解。同时将层次监督加权（HSW）适配到自回归模型，以指数方式加权递归步骤的损失，降低梯度方差并加速收敛。

Result: 在八个具有挑战性的基准上，R-Qwen持续优于先前的递归推理模型和参数量更大的LLM，同时使用可比较的可训练参数。在ARC-AGI数据集上相对于基线取得了27.6%的提升。HSW将梯度方差降低了至少50%，改善了随机梯度的信噪比并加速了收敛。

Conclusion: 递归推理机制与预训练语言模型先验是互补的方法，可共同提升符号谜题求解能力。

Abstract: Hard symbolic-reasoning tasks such as Sudoku, maze pathfinding, and ARC remain challenging for LLMs due to their fixed-depth autoregressive reasoning, which limits systematic search, refinement, and backtracking. While recursive models such as Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) and Tiny Recursive Model (TRM) address this limitation through iterative latent-state refinement, they are typically task-specific and do not leverage pretrained language priors. We propose R-Qwen, a recursive reasoning framework built upon a pretrained Qwen backbone. R-Qwen repeatedly refines a candidate solution through programmatic self-recursion and deep supervision, combining the structured iterative computation of recursive models with the linguistic and reasoning priors of pretrained LLMs. We further adapt Hierarchical Supervision Weighting (HSW) to autoregressive models by exponentially weighting losses across recursive steps. HSW reduces gradient variance by at least 50\%, improves the signal-to-noise ratio of stochastic gradients, and accelerates convergence. Across eight challenging benchmarks, R-Qwen consistently outperforms prior recursive reasoning models and substantially larger LLMs while using a comparable number of trainable parameters. Notably, on ARC-AGI dataset, our model achieves a 27.6\% improvement over the baseline, highlighting the effectiveness of recursive refinement for general symbolic reasoning. These results suggest that recursive reasoning mechanisms and pretrained language model priors are complementary approaches for improving symbolic puzzle-solving. Code and models will be released after acceptance.

</details>


### [156] [STAR-VLM: Spatiotemporal Grounding Vision-Language Models for Motion and Velocity Estimation via Automotive Radar Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01535)
*Pou-Chun Kung,Aryaman Rao,Utkrisht Sahai,Hemanth Murali,Yi Liu,Rui-Yu Lin,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-VLM，一种利用车载雷达作为无标签监督来源的框架，通过雷达的测距和Doppler测量来增强视觉语言模型（VLM）的时空推理和度量速度估计能力，在运动分类和度量速度估计任务上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升VLM时空推理能力的方法依赖复杂预处理、昂贵的人工标注或合成数据，存在可扩展性差和sim-to-real差距问题；同时这些方法虽改善了时空理解，但缺乏对动态场景的度量推理能力（如以真实单位估计物体运动）。已有的LiDAR监督方法只解决了空间感知，未直接解决时间推理。

Method: STAR-VLM利用汽车雷达（低成本、广泛部署的传感器）提供的距离和Doppler测量，作为无需人工标注的ground truth监督信号来训练VLM。雷达的测距和Doppler数据互补地提供时空监督，增强模型在动态场景中对物体运动推理和度量速度估计的能力。

Result: 在驾驶场景实验中，STAR-VLM在运动分类和度量速度估计两个任务上均取得最先进的性能，甚至超过了针对各任务专门设计的专用方法。

Conclusion: 车载雷达是一种可扩展且成本低廉的监督来源，能够有效构建具有度量感知能力的时空VLM，为真实世界自动驾驶提供支持。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are emerging as a key component of embodied intelligence, with growing applications in auto-labeling and end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing approaches for improving spatiotemporal reasoning in VLMs often rely on complex preprocessing pipelines, expensive human annotations, or synthetic data, which limit scalability and introduce potential sim-to-real gaps. Moreover, although these methods have improved spatiotemporal understanding, they still lack strong metric reasoning capabilities for dynamic scenes, such as estimating object motion in real-world units. Prior work has explored LiDAR-based metric depth supervision to enhance spatial perception, but it does not directly address temporal reasoning. We introduce STAR-VLM, an automotive radar-supervised framework that enhances spatiotemporal VLMs with motion reasoning and metric velocity estimation for autonomous driving. Automotive radar is a low-cost and widely deployed sensor that provides complementary spatiotemporal supervision through range and Doppler measurements. By leveraging these measurements as label-free ground truth during training, STAR-VLM improves the metric spatiotemporal reasoning ability of VLMs. Through experiments on driving scenarios, we show that STAR-VLM achieves state-of-the-art performance on both motion classification and metric velocity estimation, outperforming even task-specific methods designed for each task. These results highlight automotive radar as a scalable and cost-effective source of supervision for building metric-aware spatiotemporal VLMs for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [157] [Enhancing Visual Perception in Foggy Conditions via Multiclass Fog Density Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01572)
*Mohamad Mofeed Chaar,Galia Weidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶在浓雾天气下的感知问题，利用Waymo数据集合成五种雾密度等级的图像，并针对每个雾等级分别训练独立的感知模型，而非单一通用模型。实验表明，密度专属训练在极重雾场景下将召回率从0.076提升至0.232（绝对增益15.6个百分点），证明了部署多个专用模型比单一通用模型更能提升自动驾驶在恶劣可见度条件下的感知鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统虽然在过去十年发展迅速，但在浓雾等恶劣天气条件下的鲁棒感知仍是重大挑战。现有方法通常训练单一通用模型处理所有天气条件，但不同雾密度对感知性能的影响差异巨大，需要探索更有效的训练策略来应对这一难题。

Method: 基于Waymo数据集，使用迭代学习方法生成深度图像以支持雾模拟，构建五种雾密度等级（清晰、轻雾、中度雾、重雾、极重雾）的合成数据；针对每个雾密度等级分别训练独立的感知模型，与训练单一统一模型的方法进行对比评估。

Result: 密度专属训练在严重雾天条件下显著提升了感知性能。在极重雾类别中，召回率从0.076提升到0.232，绝对增益达15.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 部署多个雾密度专用的感知模型比使用单一通用模型更能提升自动驾驶在挑战性可见度条件下的感知鲁棒性。未来工作将扩展到LiDAR和雷达等多模态感知，并评估在多样化天气场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous driving (AD) systems have advanced rapidly over the past decade; however, robust perception under adverse weather conditions remains a major challenge, particularly in dense fog. In this work, we investigate fog-aware perception using synthetically generated fog data derived from the Waymo dataset. To support fog simulation, depth images are generated using an iterative learning approach. We consider five fog-density levels: clear, light fog, moderate fog, heavy fog, and very heavy fog. Instead of training a single unified model across all conditions, we train separate perception models for each fog-density level. Experimental results show that density-specific training improves performance in severe fog conditions. In particular, for the very heavy fog class, recall improves from 0.076 to 0.232, corresponding to an absolute gain of 15.6 percentage points. These findings suggest that deploying multiple specialized models, rather than a single general-purpose model, can improve perception robustness for autonomous vehicles under challenging visibility conditions. Future work will extend this strategy to additional sensing modalities, including LiDAR and radar, and evaluate generalization across diverse weather scenarios.

</details>


### [158] [D^2-4DGS: Dual-Depth Guided Sparse-Camera 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01588)
*Jijian Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D²-4DGS，一种利用双源深度先验指导的稀疏相机动态4D高斯泼溅框架，通过融合单目深度和多视图几何深度互补优势，实现稀疏视角下的高质量动态新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态4D高斯泼溅方法通常需要密集多视角视频来获得足够的几何约束，导致采集成本高、稀疏相机部署受限。减少输入视图虽然降低采集成本，但会弱化几何监督，导致结构缺失和漂浮高斯伪影。深度先验虽能提供几何线索，但单一来源无法同时保证稠密覆盖和几何可靠性。

Method: 提出D²-4DGS框架，包含四个关键组件：(1) 将对齐单目深度估计与有效的多视图几何深度进行一致性验证，识别可靠几何锚点；(2) 利用验证后的锚点进行一致性感知剪枝和深度监督；(3) 验证的几何深度和对齐的单目估计为欠重建区域提供候选几何进行稠密化；(4) RGB-D联合优化提升外观保真度和几何一致性。

Result: 在全部九个数据集-视角组合设置中，D²-4DGS均取得最高PSNR，相比各设置中最优的对比方法平均提升1.33 dB。

Conclusion: 双源深度先验的互补性能够有效缓解稀疏相机下动态4D高斯泼溅的几何监督不足问题，在降低采集成本的同时保持高质量的重建和渲染效果。

Abstract: Dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as an efficient representation for dynamic novel view synthesis through explicit scene modeling and real-time rendering. However, existing methods typically require dense multi-view videos for sufficient geometric constraints, making capture expensive and limiting sparse-camera deployment. Reducing input views lowers acquisition cost but weakens geometry supervision, often causing missing structures and floating Gaussians. Depth priors provide geometric cues, yet no single source offers both dense coverage and reliable geometry. Monocular depth provides dense structure but is scale-ambiguous and locally biased, whereas multi-view geometric depth provides incomplete anchors consistent with the reconstruction coordinate system. To exploit their complementarity, we propose D$^2$-4DGS, a sparse-camera dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting framework guided by dual-source depth priors. We align monocular estimates with valid multi-view geometric depths and verify their consistency to identify reliable geometric anchors. These verified anchors support consistency-aware pruning and depth supervision, while verified geometric depths and aligned mono-only estimates provide candidate geometry for densification in under-reconstructed regions. Finally, RGB-D joint optimization improves appearance fidelity and geometric consistency under sparse-view supervision. Across all nine dataset--view settings, D$^2$-4DGS achieves the highest PSNR, improving by 1.33 dB on average over the best competing method in each setting.

</details>


### [159] [When Measurement Conventions Masquerade as Calibration Gains in Cardiac Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01602)
*Dang P. M. Cao,Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了心脏数字孪生中射血分数（EF）校准研究的基准约定问题。研究发现看似有效的相位条件化改进实为测量伪影所致——单平面金标准EF比CAMUS双平面临床EF高6.30个百分点，解释了几乎所有基线偏差。通过匹配参考分析、预注册的EchoNet-Dynamic复制以及提出的"约定感知EF审计协议"，作者区分了真实的观测算子校准与测量伪影，并揭示了相位条件化导致的排名逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏数字孪生通过观测算子将临床图像转化为生理测量值，但现有校准研究通常假设固定的参考约定，忽略了不同参考标准（如单平面vs双平面EF）之间的系统性偏差，可能导致虚假的模型改进结论。

Method: 通过对四个共享骨干的超声心动图EF前端进行对照分析：1) 比较相位条件化前后的CAMUS基线偏差；2) 采用匹配参考（matched-reference）分析，对比单平面金标准EF与CAMUS双平面临床EF；3) 进行预注册的EchoNet-Dynamic复制实验，将对齐到心尖四腔平面的提取器与释放数据配对；4) 量化血流动力学效应、共形残差宽度预算和EF分层变化，提出"约定感知EF审计协议"。

Result: 相位条件化看似消除了CAMUS基线偏差，但匹配参考分析拒绝了这一结论：所有模型的单平面金标准EF误差在统计上无显著差异；单平面金标准EF比CAMUS双平面临床EF高出6.30个百分点，解释了几乎所有基线偏差。EchoNet-Dynamic复制消除了基线高估并逆转了CAMUS排名。

Conclusion: EF校准中的性能差异可能来自参考约定的混淆而非真实的观测算子改进。提出的约定感知EF审计协议能够将真实的观测算子校准从测量伪影中分离出来，为心脏数字孪生校准研究提供了更严谨的评估框架。

Abstract: Cardiac digital twins convert clinical images into physiological measurements through observation operators, yet calibration studies often assume a fixed reference convention. Across four shared-backbone echocardiographic EF front-ends, phase conditioning appears to remove CAMUS baseline bias. Matched-reference analysis rejects this gain: singleplane ground-truth EF error is statistically indistinguishable across models, while single-plane ground-truth EF exceeds CAMUS biplane clinical EF by +6.30 points, explaining nearly all baseline bias. A prespecified EchoNet-Dynamic replication, with released data and our extractor aligned to the apical four-chamber plane, removes baseline overestimation and reverses the CAMUS ranking. We also quantify haemodynamic effects, conformal residual-width budgets, and EF-stratum changes, yielding a Convention-Aware EF Audit protocol that separates genuine observation operator calibration from measurement artefacts. GitHub: EjectionFraction-Bias-in-Cardiac-Digital-Twin.git

</details>


### [160] [Linear Multi-Timescale Retention as a Memory-Efficient Vision-Language Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01614)
*Ashfak Yeafi,Mehedi Hasan,Md Khairul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LIA-MTR模块，一种线性多时间尺度记忆保留机制，通过ELU特征映射、自适应写门控和对数线性分布的循环衰减，将视觉序列压缩为有界记忆状态，实现了严格O(N)的序列交互复杂度，支持无限上下文的高分辨率视觉语言处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在处理高分辨率图像时面临Softmax多头注意力O(N²)内存复杂度的计算瓶颈，而简单的MLP替代虽然实现了O(N)缩放，但丢失了空间序列路由能力，导致全局场景理解和物体恒存性严重退化。

Method: LIA-MTR模块整合了基于ELU的正特征映射、自适应写门控以及对数线性分布的循环衰减机制，将连续视觉序列数学压缩为有界记忆状态，实现O(N)的序列交互复杂度。通过LLM预训练损失函数对视觉编码器和可训练的桥接模块进行联合优化。

Result: LIA-MTR在16,000 token检索中无"Lost in the Middle"退化；硬件测试中可原生处理262,144个视觉块仅占用11.2GB显存（标准MHA在16,384块即内存溢出）；指令微调665K样本后，在MME基准上以71.00%显著超越MLP基线的68.11%，物体恒存性提升10%，全局语义提取更优。

Conclusion: LIA-MTR为无限上下文的视觉语言集成建立了数学上严谨、计算复杂度平坦的基础，在保持O(N)线性复杂度的同时不牺牲空间序列路由能力，为高效高分辨率视觉理解提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) face a critical computational bottleneck when processing high-resolution imagery due to the $O(N^2)$ memory complexity of Softmax Multi-Head Attention (MHA). While substituting MHA with independent Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) achieves $O(N)$ scaling, it strips the architecture of spatial sequence routing, severely degrading global scene understanding and object permanence. In this paper, we propose the Linear Multi-Timescale Retention (LIA-MTR) module, a memory-efficient cross-modal bridge. By integrating an ELU-based positive feature mapping with adaptive write-gating and log-linearly distributed recurrent decays, LIA-MTR mathematically compresses continuous visual sequences into bounded memory states. Theoretical analysis proves the architecture operates with strict $O(N)$ sequence-interaction complexity. Empirically, synthetic retrieval evaluations demonstrate that LIA-MTR flawlessly routes context across 16,000 tokens, eliminating the "Lost in the Middle" degradation typical of naive linear attention. Hardware benchmarking reveals infinite-context scaling capabilities, natively processing 262,144 visual patches within an 11.2 GB VRAM footprint, whereas standard MHA suffers out-of-memory failure at 16,384 patches. Furthermore, following instruction tuning on 665K conversational samples, LIA-MTR significantly outperforms an industry-standard MLP baseline on the MME benchmark (71.00% vs. 68.11%), driven by a 10% absolute improvement in object permanence and superior global semantic extraction. This work establishes a mathematically rigorous, computationally flat foundation for infinite-context Vision-Language integration.

</details>


### [161] [Motion Beyond Morphology: Bootstrapping Cross-Category Motion Transfer from Abstract Motion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01628)
*Zhixue Fang,Zhimin Zhang,Bi'an Du,Zijie Meng,Yan Zhou,Wei Hu,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"超越形态的运动"（Motion Beyond Morphology）视角，通过两阶段框架实现跨形态差异的视频运动迁移，并构建了OpenVMT数据集与基准用于训练和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频运动迁移方法依赖固定的结构对应关系，当参考视频与目标对象在形态、关节结构或变形机制上差异较大时，这种对应关系定义失效，限制了运动迁移的泛化能力。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段学习互补的多粒度抽象运动视图，并利用这些视图引导生成跨类别的视频对，保留跨形态可迁移的运动动态；第二阶段将这种监督内化到直接的参考视频条件生成中，推理时无需显式运动提取。

Result: 在OpenVMT-Bench上（涵盖同类别、近类别和远类别的间隙）的实验表明，该方法在运动保真度和目标保持方面均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过放松固定结构对应的约束，将运动定义为超越形态的可迁移动态，能够有效支持跨形态差异的运动迁移，并为该任务提供了统一的数据集与评估基准。

Abstract: Video motion transfer aims to animate a target object using dynamics from a reference video. Existing formulations largely rely on fixed structural correspondence, which becomes ill-defined when reference and target objects differ substantially in morphology, articulation, or deformation mechanisms. We introduce Motion Beyond Morphology, a perspective that seeks to transfer motion beyond fixed structural correspondence, by preserving dynamics that remain meaningful across different target morphologies. To realize this, we propose a two-stage framework. Stage~I learns complementary multi-granularity abstract motion views and uses them to bootstrap cross-category video pairs that preserve transferable dynamics across diverse morphologies. Stage~II internalizes this supervision into direct reference-video-conditioned generation, removing the need for explicit motion extraction at inference. We further introduce OpenVMT-Dataset and OpenVMT-Bench for training and evaluating image- and text-conditioned motion transfer across Same, Near, and Far category gaps, and plan to release both upon acceptance. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art motion fidelity and target preservation. Project page: https://miniz233.github.io/MotionBeyondMorphology/

</details>


### [162] [Mitigating Visual Degradation in MLLMs via Spatial-Spectral Visual Anchor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01635)
*Qianlong Yang,Bowen Ye,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Wenke Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Yulei Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间-光谱视觉锚点学习（SSVAL）方法，通过视觉锚点提示注入（VAPI）将外部视觉基础模型的知识吸收为稳定锚点，并引入空间域和频域表示对齐损失，解决多模态大语言模型在视觉指令微调后内部表示发生漂移导致视觉感知退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉指令微调后，内部表示在推理过程中会快速偏离原始语义状态，导致严重的信息退化。现有方法尝试利用外部视觉基础模型对齐内部表示，但直接对齐虽能增强视觉语义，却无法缓解表示漂移问题。

Method: SSVAL的核心是视觉锚点提示注入（VAPI），在训练阶段引入从外部视觉基础模型吸收丰富知识的提示，使其在推理阶段作为稳定视觉锚点来缓解表示漂移。此外，还引入辅助的空间域和频域表示对齐损失，在中间LLM层提供互补的视觉特定监督。

Result: 大量实验表明SSVAL在性能上显著优于现有方法，代码已在项目页面上公开。

Conclusion: 通过将外部视觉模型知识注入为稳定锚点并结合多域监督，SSVAL有效解决了MLLM视觉表示漂移问题，为多模态模型的视觉感知增强提供了新思路。

Abstract: Despite the progress of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), they continue to exhibit deficiencies in visual perception. Following visual instruction tuning, internal MLLM representations rapidly deviate from their original semantic states during inference, causing severe information degradation. While existing methods attempt to leverage external vision foundation models (VFMs) to align internal representations, we find that direct alignment with VFMs enhances visual semantics but fails to mitigate representation deviation. To address this, we propose Spatial-Spectral Visual Anchor Learning (SSVAL). The core of SSVAL is Visual Anchor Prompt Injection (VAPI), which introduces prompts that absorb rich knowledge from external VFMs during training, enabling them to serve as stable visual anchors that mitigate representation deviation during inference. Additionally, we incorporate auxiliary spatial and frequency-domain representation alignment losses to provide complementary vision-specific supervision at intermediate LLM layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SSVAL significantly outperforms existing methods. Code are available on our \href{https://msls38.github.io/SSVAL/}{project page}.

</details>


### [163] [Dynamic Resolution Routing for Efficient Egocentric Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01638)
*Huixin Sun,Wangbo Zhao,Fanyue Wei,Qiuxia Lin,Pengzhan Sun,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartRes是一个在像素空间通过动态分辨率路由进行效率优化的框架，用于解决第一人称视角视觉定位中高分辨率输入成本过高的问题。它通过低分辨率全局编码和轻量路由器的配合，在物体中心区域激活高分辨率补丁，同时引入边界正则化路由目标来应对前景-背景不平衡，在Ego4D和EgoIntention上实现了最多67%的视觉token缩减和1.66倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视角（第一人称）视觉定位需要高分辨率输入来定位小物体，但多模态大语言模型在此领域的扩展受到视觉token处理成本过高的限制。现有的基于token缩减的高效策略无法可靠地选择以物体为中心的空间证据。

Method: 提出SmartRes框架，在像素空间进行动态分辨率路由优化。首先编码低分辨率视图获取全局上下文，使用轻量路由器在物体中心区域激活高分辨率补丁，并构建保序的视觉序列。同时引入边界正则化路由目标（margin-regularized routing objective），增强前景-背景logit分离并提高前景召回率。

Result: 在Ego4D和EgoIntention数据集上，SmartRes最多减少67%的视觉token，同时保留86.4%的全分辨率性能，推理速度比最先进的token缩减方法快1.66倍且准确率更高。在小物体定位上表现出色。

Conclusion: SmartRes通过在像素空间而非token空间进行效率优化，有效解决了第一人称视觉定位中高分辨率成本与物体定位精度之间的矛盾，展示了其在第一人称应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Egocentric visual grounding requires high-resolution inputs to localize small objects. However, scaling Multimodal Large Language Models to this domain is constrained by the excessive cost of visual token processing. We identify that current efficient strategies based on token reduction are unreliable for selecting object-centric spatial evidence. To overcome this, we propose SmartRes, a framework that performs efficiency optimization in the pixel space via dynamic resolution routing. SmartRes first encodes a low-resolution view for global context and uses a lightweight router to activate high-resolution patches in object-centric regions and constructs an order-preserving visual sequence. To further enable robust routing under severe foreground-background imbalance, we introduce a margin-regularized routing objective that increases foreground-background logit separation and improves foreground recall. Experiments on Ego4D and EgoIntention show that SmartRes reduces visual tokens by up to 67% while retaining 86.4% of full-resolution performance, and achieves up to 1.66X faster inference than state-of-the-art token reduction methods with higher accuracy. Furthermore, strong performance on small object grounding indicates the effectiveness of SmartRes towards egocentric applications. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [164] [StreamTalk: Streaming Co-Speech Gesture Generation with Key-Pose Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01643)
*Xiangyue Zhang,Jianfang Li,Jiaxu Zhang,Kaixing Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamTalk是一个闭环的实时共语手势生成框架，通过周期性的"生成-检索-精炼"循环（Generate-Retrieve-Refine）来解决开放循环流式方法中误差累积导致的漂移问题。它利用尾部关键姿态作为目标锚点来限制漂移，采用随机锚点掩码训练和部分感知DiT来分离手部、身体和平移流，在BEAT2上达到SOTA的FGD指标，以76 FPS实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式共语手势生成方法是开放循环的，每个片段依赖过去的上下文，但模型无法检查或修正其轨迹，小的误差会累积导致长时间序列中的漂移。作者观察到这种失败主要源于缺乏前向约束，而非短片段生成质量差。

Method: 提出StreamTalk闭环框架，包含周期性的生成-检索-精炼循环：1) 流式姿态引导生成模块先预测粗略片段；2) 从说话者特定的动作数据库中检索一个合理的尾部姿态；3) 使用该姿态精炼当前片段后再继续下一个窗口。训练时使用随机锚点掩码（Stochastic Anchor Masking）随机遮蔽姿态和平移帧，教模型从稀疏边界条件恢复完整运动。使用部分感知的DiT（part-aware DiT）分离手部、身体和平移流以减少全局位移和局部关节运动之间的干扰。

Result: 在BEAT2数据集上，StreamTalk取得了SOTA的FGD（Fréchet Gesture Distance）分数，相比开放循环基线显著减少了长时程漂移，并且以76 FPS的速度实时运行。

Conclusion: 通过引入前向锚点约束的闭环设计，StreamTalk有效解决了流式手势生成中的漂移问题，表明尾部关键姿态锚定是限制误差累积的有效策略，同时实时性能满足实际应用需求。

Abstract: Real-time co-speech gesture generation must produce 3D motion clip by clip as speech arrives. Existing streaming methods are open-loop: each clip depends on past context, but the model cannot check or correct its trajectory. Small errors therefore accumulate and cause drift over long sequences. We observe that this failure is mainly caused by the lack of a forward constraint rather than poor short-clip quality. A plausible key pose at the end of each clip provides a destination anchor that limits drift. Based on this observation, we propose StreamTalk, a closed-loop framework with a periodic generate-retrieve-refine cycle. Streaming Pose-Guided Generation first predicts a coarse clip, retrieves a plausible tail pose from a speaker-specific motion database, and refines the clip using this pose before continuing to the next window. During training, Stochastic Anchor Masking randomly masks pose and translation frames, teaching the model to recover complete motion from sparse boundary conditions. A part-aware DiT separates hand, body, and translation streams to reduce interference between global displacement and local articulation. On BEAT2, StreamTalk achieves state-of-the-art FGD, reduces long-horizon drift relative to open-loop baselines, and runs in real time at 76 FPS. Project page: https://xiangyue-zhang.github.io/StreamTalk/.

</details>


### [165] [CRAFT: Compression via Recursive Adaptive Fusion of Video Tokens for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01644)
*Yu Chen,Xiaohong Li,Xiaole Wang,Jianjin Zhang,Jun Sun,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRAFT通过解耦参数无关的token选择与可学习的token融合，实现递归自适应视频token压缩，在约8倍压缩率下保留约97%的模型精度并显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解中视觉token数量庞大导致prefill阶段计算和内存成本急剧上升，现有token压缩方法要么采用启发式无训练方法缺乏内容自适应性，要么引入需要昂贵对齐训练的新模块，效率与自适应性之间的权衡问题尚未解决。

Method: CRAFT递归地合并token，通过解耦参数无关的token选择与可学习的token融合：全局相似度决定合并哪些token，位置感知加权模块和内容自适应通道门控学习如何融合。整个压缩流程与查询无关，每个保留token是原始token的线性组合，从而保留真实的时空坐标并与预训练语言模型的输入分布保持一致。

Result: 在多个代表性视频基准上，CRAFT一致优于此前最先进的token压缩方法。在约8倍压缩率下，保留了骨干模型约97%的平均准确率，同时展现出显著的效率提升。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过将token选择与融合解耦，在保持高压缩比的同时保留了关键细节，有效解决了视频token压缩中效率与自适应性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: In video understanding, vision-language models (VLMs) must ingest massive numbers of visual tokens, causing the computational and memory cost of the prefill stage to rise sharply. Such visual sequences are highly redundant along the spatio-temporal dimension, yet a high compression ratio is often accompanied by the loss of critical details. Existing token-compression methods either employ heuristic, training-free compression with limited content adaptivity or introduce additional modules that require expensive alignment training, leaving the trade-off between efficiency and adaptivity unresolved. To alleviate this limitation, we propose CRAFT: Compression via Recursive Adaptive Fusion of Video Tokens. CRAFT recursively merges tokens by decoupling parameter-free token selection from learnable token fusion: global similarity determines which tokens to merge, while a position-aware weighting module and a content-adaptive channel-wise gate learn how to fuse them. The whole compression pipeline is query-agnostic. Because every retained token is a linear combination of the original tokens, CRAFT preserves their true spatio-temporal coordinates and stays aligned with the pre-trained language model's input distribution. Experiments on multiple representative video benchmarks show that CRAFT consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art token-compression methods. At about $8\times$ compression, it retains roughly $97\%$ of the backbone's average accuracy and shows significant efficiency improvement.

</details>


### [166] [StreamSplat: Streaming Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01659)
*Changhao Song,Yuxuan Wang,Qibiao Li,Youcheng Cai,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamSplat是一个流式前馈3D高斯泼溅框架，通过体素对齐因果缓存（VACC）增量维护场景状态，实现无需未来视角或全场景上下文的在线新视角合成，可扩展到数百乃至上千个视图的长输入流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D高斯泼溅方法假设固定的上下文视图集合并联合处理，无法适应在线场景中视图逐序到达、场景需因果更新的需求，限制了在长输入流场景中的应用。

Method: 提出StreamSplat框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) VACC（Voxel-Aligned Causal Cache）——以内存受限的体素结构存储历史3D标记，内存随探索几何增长而非流长度；(2) HPDA（History-Projected Depth Anchoring）——将缓存几何投影为深度引导，用于当前代价体估计；(3) CGFI（Cache-Guided Feature Injection）——将缓存潜在证据注入高斯标记回归过程。

Result: 在DL3DV、RealEstate10K和ScanNet上，StreamSplat在稀疏因果输入下与最先进的前馈3DGS方法保持竞争力；在256、512和1024视图的长输入流上，固定视图基线内存耗尽，而StreamSplat可持续运行并获得随观测增多的新视角合成质量提升。

Conclusion: StreamSplat证明了流式前馈3DGS的可行性，通过内存有界的缓存设计使记忆随场景几何增长而非流长度，实现了无需未来信息的长流在线新视角合成。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel-view synthesis without per-scene optimization, but most existing methods assume a fixed set of context views and process them jointly. This limits their applicability to online scenarios where calibrated views arrive sequentially and the scene must be updated causally. We present \emph{StreamSplat}, a streaming feed-forward 3DGS framework that incrementally maintains a persistent geometry-grounded scene state and decodes it into renderable 3D Gaussians after each input chunk. StreamSplat centers on a \textbf{Voxel-Aligned Causal Cache (VACC)}, which stores historical 3D tokens in a memory-bounded voxel structure so that memory grows with explored scene geometry rather than stream length. To better reuse history during causal prediction, we introduce \textbf{History-Projected Depth Anchoring (HPDA)} to project cached geometry as depth guidance for current cost-volume estimation, and \textbf{Cache-Guided Feature Injection (CGFI)} to inject cached latent evidence into Gaussian-token regression. Experiments on DL3DV, RealEstate10K, and ScanNet show that StreamSplat remains competitive with state-of-the-art feed-forward 3DGS methods under sparse causal inputs, despite not using future views or full-scene context. More importantly, it scales to long input streams with 256, 512, and 1024 views where fixed-view baselines run out of memory, yielding sustained improvements in novel-view synthesis quality as more observations arrive. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [167] [Ground, Cover, and Refine: Evidence-Centric Frame Selection for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01660)
*Fan Wei,Siru Zhong,Runmin Dong,Miao Yang,Zhaoyang Luo,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GCR框架，一种无需训练的固定预算帧选择方法，用于长视频问答。GCR通过Ground（将时间戳文本转化为时间事件并渲染到对应帧）、Cover（补充直接视觉锚点并应用全局最大边际相关性保持多样性）、Refine（回顾遗漏的时间区域并用真实帧质心替换最弱的可修订帧）三个步骤，协同优化文本和视觉证据的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频QA方法存在两个关键局限：一是单次选帧容易聚集在局部相关峰值附近，一旦预算耗尽遗漏的证据无法恢复；二是文本与视觉证据之间的对齐较弱。需要一种更优的固定预算帧选择策略。

Method: GCR框架包含三个核心步骤：Ground将时间戳文本转化为时间事件，选择查询相关的真实帧锚点，并将每个事件文本渲染到其时间对齐的帧上；Cover通过全局最大边际相关性补充直接视觉锚点以保留多样化上下文；Refine回顾被遗漏的时间区域，仅在质心能提供更大证据价值时，用真实帧质心替换最弱的可修订上下文帧。

Result: 在LongVideoBench和Video-MME两个基准上，使用三种7B骨干模型和8/32/64帧预算，GCR均取得一致改进。使用7B LLaVA-OV骨干和32帧时，GCR在两项基准上分别达到64.25%和62.15%，超过最强复现基线2.54和1.93个百分点。

Conclusion: GCR作为无需训练、不修改VLM架构的框架，通过联合证据策展的方式有效解决了长视频QA中固定预算帧选择的问题，在多个骨干模型和帧预算设置下均表现稳定优越。

Abstract: Long-video question answering requires identifying sparse yet critical evidence from videos containing thousands of frames under a constrained visual-token budget. Existing methods either select query-aware frames in a single pass or rely on timestamped text solely as retrieval guidance, leading to two key limitations. First, selected frames tend to cluster around local relevance peaks, and once the budget is exhausted, omitted evidence cannot be recovered. Second, textual and visual evidence remain weakly aligned. We propose GCR, a training-free framework that casts fixed-budget frame selection as a joint evidence curation problem. Ground converts timestamped text into temporal events, selects query-relevant real frame anchors, and renders each event text onto its temporally aligned frame. Cover supplements grounded events with direct visual anchors for complementary visual evidence and applies global maximal marginal relevance to preserve diverse context. Refine revisits omitted temporal regions and replaces the weakest revisable context frame with a real-frame medoid---but only when the medoid offers greater evidence value. GCR maintains a fixed number of chronologically ordered frames and requires no VLM training or architectural modification. Experiments on LongVideoBench and Video-MME, across three 7B backbones and frame budgets of 8, 32, and 64, demonstrate consistent improvements in long-video QA. With the 7B LLaVA-OV backbone and 32 frames, GCR achieves 64.25% and 62.15% on the two benchmarks, outperforming the strongest reproduced baselines by 2.54 and 1.93 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [168] [FairForensics: Seeing Expressions and Parsing Demographics via Vision-Language Modeling for Generalizable Fair Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01661)
*Yaning Zhang,Jiao Wu,Zan Gao,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FairForensics，一个基于视觉-语言模型的公平深度伪造检测框架，通过构建人口统计学平衡基准数据，结合表情感知的伪造检测和人口统计学感知的公平性正则化，在泛化性和公平性上均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平深度伪造检测方法在不同伪造手法上的泛化能力差，且对人口统计学群体的公平性不足。现有方法通常在人口统计学不平衡的数据上开发和评估，导致对少数群体的预测存在偏差。

Method: 1) 构建人口统计学平衡的FDD基准，支持在平衡与不平衡两种人群场景下训练和评估；2) FairForensics包含两个核心模块：表情伪造泛化增强模块，利用表情编码器和表情感知视觉编码器捕捉高层的表情引导伪造模式，并通过身份感知补丁扰动模块减少身份偏差；人口统计学感知公平性正则化模块，利用人口统计学引导的语言编码器提取全局语言嵌入，通过视觉-语言对齐解耦伪造特征与人口统计学信息，并设计人口感知原型公平性目标函数实现类间可分离性和类内对齐。

Result: 在构建的平衡人口统计学基准上，FairForensics在泛化能力和公平性两方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过引入人口统计学平衡的数据基准和双管齐下的公平性增强机制（表情感知泛化+人口统计学感知正则化），FairForensics有效解决了深度伪造检测中的泛化性和公平性难题，为公平伪造检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The challenge of fair deepfake detection (FDD) has attracted increasing attention. Existing fairness-enhanced detectors often suffer from suboptimal generalization to unseen manipulations and fairness across demographic groups. They are typically developed and evaluated on demographically imbalanced distributions, resulting in biased predictions toward minority groups. In this paper, we construct a novel demographically balanced FDD benchmark to train and evaluate the fairness of detectors under both balanced and imbalanced population scenarios. Additionally, we introduce a novel expression and demographic perceptual vision-language model, termed FairForensics, for generalizable fair deepfake detection. FairForensics conducts face forgery generalization enhancement and demographic-aware fairness regularization. During face forgery generalization enhancement, built upon the novel observation of significant distribution differences between pristine and forged expression vectors, we design an expression encoder to capture high-level expression-guided forgery patterns, and an expression-perceptual visual encoder that integrates global appearance and expression forgery features while mitigating identity bias using an identity-aware patch perturbation module. Under demographic-aware fairness regularization, we propose a demographic-guided language encoder to extract population-aware global language embeddings, which boosts the decoupling of forgery features from demographic information via vision-language alignment. We devise a population-aware prototype fairness objective to enforce both inter-class separability and intra-class alignment across demographic subgroups. Extensive experiments on our balanced demographic benchmark show that our method achieves the state-of-the-art in terms of generalization and fairness.

</details>


### [169] [Few-Shot Concept Prompt Learning for Segmentation Foundation Models via Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01663)
*Rahul Venkataramani,Rachana Sathish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FS-CPL方法，通过从少量图像-掩码对中学习连续概念提示嵌入来替代自然语言提示，解决提示式分割基础模型在医学影像中性能不佳的结构性局限，在四个公开基准上Dice提升最高达+0.62。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示式分割基础模型（如SAM3、Medical SAM3）虽承诺通过自然语言实现医学影像的少样本交互式分割，但在临床任务上表现远低于预期。作者认为这并非预训练不足或提示措辞的问题，而是图像-文本配对监督稀缺这一结构性局限所致，且该局限与自然语言作为控制信号本身有关。

Method: 提出Few-Shot Concept Prompt Learning（FS-CPL），从K个图像-掩码对的小支持集出发，通过掩码监督学习一个连续的概念提示嵌入p*∈R^{T×d}，同时保持编码器-解码器骨干网络冻结。

Result: 在四个公开基准（BUSI、HC18、TN3K、CVC-Clinic）上，覆盖超声和内镜两种模态，FS-CPL相较标准文本提示的绝对Dice提升最高达+0.62；方法具有骨干无关性，在vanilla SAM3和Medical SAM3上均有效。

Conclusion: 视觉概念提示与领域内预训练具有互补性，从目标分布直接学习视觉化提示可以恢复自然语言提示所损失的性能，无需额外的图像-文本数据或骨干网络重训练。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation foundation models (FMs) such as SAM3 and Medical SAM3 promise few-shot, interactively-specified segmentation for medical imaging through a natural language interface, yet their performance on clinical tasks falls well short of this promise. We posit that this shortfall is not an artefact of insufficient medical pretraining or imperfect prompt phrasing, but a structural limitation that will persist in any domain where paired image-text supervision is scarce, as it is across most clinical modalities. We further hypothesize that the limitation is specific to natural language as a control signal: a visually grounded prompt, learned directly from the target distribution, should recover the lost performance without additional image-text data or backbone retraining. We propose Few-Shot Concept Prompt Learning (FS-CPL), which learns a continuous concept prompt embedding $\mathbf{p}^* \in \mathbb{R}^{T \times d}$ from a small support set of $K$ image--mask pairs via mask supervision, with the encoder-decoder backbone frozen. Across four public benchmarks spanning ultrasound and endoscopy (BUSI, HC18, TN3K, CVC-Clinic), FS-CPL delivers absolute Dice improvements of up to $+0.62$ over canonical text prompts and is \emph{backbone-agnostic}: it lifts both vanilla SAM3 and the domain-specifically pretrained Medical SAM3, showing that visual concept prompting is complementary to in-domain pretraining.

</details>


### [170] [FAU at ImageCLEF 2026 Task on Multimodal Reasoning Robust Candidate Scoring and Concise Multilingual Visual Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01664)
*Mohamed Basem,Vincent Christlein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ImageCLEF 2026多模态推理系统的设计，针对视觉多项选择题（Visual MCQ）和视觉开放问答（Visual OpenQA）两个子任务。核心发现是输出控制与模型选择同等重要，通过在MCQ中采用候选标签logit评分替代自由生成，以及在OpenQA中应用图像增强、简洁提示、确定性解码和后处理，无需任务特定训练即取得了MCQ第三名和OpenQA第一名的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ImageCLEF 2026多模态推理挑战要求对多语言教育性和科学性图像（包含密集文本、图表、表格、公式和单位）进行可靠推理，同时需严格遵守答案格式。作者旨在探索在不进行任务特定模型微调的情况下，如何通过推理工程策略提升视觉语言模型（VLM）在严格格式约束下的表现。

Method: 对于Visual MCQ子任务，采用直接候选标签评分方法——从视觉语言模型的logits中获取候选标签得分，替代脆弱的自由文本生成，并通过分数融合和投票组合互补的多次运行结果。对于Visual OpenQA子任务，采用图像增强、简洁最终答案提示、确定性解码以及针对性的后处理来去除推理痕迹和格式伪影。

Result: 无需任务特定模型训练，官方提交在Visual MCQ中获得第三名（准确率0.7108），在Visual OpenQA中获得第一名（COMET 0.6488，BLEU 0.1391，ROUGE L 0.2762，METEOR 0.2383）。

Conclusion: 结果表明推理工程具有重要实用价值：细致的评分策略、模型集成、提示设计和输出清理可以将强大的VLM转化为可靠的竞赛系统，强调了输出控制与模型选择同等重要的核心发现。

Abstract: We present our ImageCLEF 2026 Multimodal Reasoning system for the Visual Multiple Choice Question Answering (Visual MCQ) and Visual Open Question Answering (Visual OpenQA) subtasks. The challenge requires reliable reasoning over multilingual educational and scientific images with dense text, diagrams, charts, tables, formulas, and units, while enforcing strict answer formats. Our central finding is that robust output control is as important as model choice. For Visual MCQ, we replace fragile free-form generation with direct candidate label scoring from vision-language model logits, then combine complementary runs through score fusion and voting. For Visual OpenQA, we use image enhancement, concise final answer prompting, deterministic decoding, and targeted post-processing to remove reasoning traces and formatting artifacts. Without task-specific model training, our official submissions achieved third place in Visual MCQ with 0.7108 accuracy and first place in Visual OpenQA with 0.6488 COMET, 0.1391 BLEU, 0.2762 ROUGE L, and 0.2383 METEOR. The results highlight the practical value of inference engineering: careful scoring, ensembling, prompting, and cleanup can turn strong VLMs into reliable competition systems.

</details>


### [171] [Generative Brownian Bridge Diffusion In Motion Space For Enhanced Myocardial Strain Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01677)
*Rishov Paul,Frederick H. Epstein,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于布朗桥扩散模型的生成式框架，用于从常规采集的心脏磁共振（CMR）图像中合成高质量的运动应变值，从而在无需专门且昂贵的成像采集的情况下实现精确的心脏功能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CMR心肌应变分析面临两大挑战：传统方法需要人工后处理且区域精度不佳，而先进应变成像技术成本高昂且需要专门采集。作者希望利用生成模型的力量，从常规CMR序列中合成高质量的运动应变值，以解决精度与成本之间的矛盾。

Method: 作者提出了一种新颖的布朗桥扩散模型，在运动空间中学习概率映射，将标准CMR运动（由广泛采用的配准方法估计得到）转换为高精度运动（由先进应变成像技术提供）。模型以对应CMR图像为条件，以提升生成过程中解剖结构的保真度。

Result: 在大规模多中心CMR数据集（包含配对的标准电影CMR和先进应变成像采集）上的实验表明，与现有基于学习的方法相比，该框架显著提高了标准CMR运动预测和应变分析的准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究代表了开发经济高效、临床可部署的AI心脏功能评估工具的新范式，可在繁忙的临床工作流程中以增强的应变精度实现心脏功能评估，代码已公开。

Abstract: Myocardial strain analysis of cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) images provides an important tool for evaluating cardiac function. However, current techniques require either human-adjusted post-processing with suboptimal regional accuracy, or specialized and costly imaging acquisitions. In this paper, we propose to leverage the power of generative models to synthesize high-quality motion-derived strain values from routinely acquired CMR sequences. Specifically, we develop a novel Brownian bridge diffusion model in motion space to learn the probabilistic mapping between standard CMR motion estimated from widely adopted registration methods and highly accurate motion provided by advanced strain imaging techniques. To promote the fidelity of anatomical structure in the generation process, our model is conditioned on the corresponding CMR images. We validate our method on large-scale multi-center CMR datasets including subjects of paired standard cine CMR and advanced strain imaging acquisitions. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves the accuracy of motion prediction and strain analysis from standard CMRs compared to existing learning-based approaches. Our research represents a new paradigm for potentially developing cost-effective, clinically deployable AI tools for cardiac function assessment with enhanced strain accuracy in busy clinical workflows. Our code is publicly available at anonymous.4open.science/r/Brownian-Bridge-strain-analysis-1140.

</details>


### [172] [Generative AI and Foundation Models in Medical Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01686)
*Masahiro Oda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文概述了生成式AI（扩散模型用于图像生成、大语言模型用于文本生成）及其在医疗支持中的应用，并探讨了基础模型的构建方法及其在医学领域的应用前景，最后提出利用国家数据和计算资源开发医学基础模型的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和基础模型的快速发展正在深刻改变医疗图像处理及整个医疗AI开发的范式，本文旨在梳理这些技术及其在医疗领域的应用现状与未来方向。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，分别介绍图像生成AI中的扩散模型（如DALL-E 3、Stable Diffusion）和文本生成AI中的大语言模型（如ChatGPT、Gemini），并讨论基础模型的构建方法及其在医疗领域的应用。

Result: 文章系统性地呈现了扩散模型和LLM在医学支持中的典型应用案例，并阐述了基础模型在医疗领域中的潜在价值，提出了基于国家数据和计算资源开发医学专用基础模型的思路。

Conclusion: 生成式AI和基础模型为医疗支持带来范式转变，充分利用国家层面的数据和算力资源有望开发出高性能的医学基础模型，推动医疗AI的发展。

Abstract: In recent years, generative AI has attracted significant public attention, and its use has been rapidly expanding across a wide range of domains. From creative tasks such as text summarization, idea generation, and source code generation, to the streamlining of medical support tasks like diagnostic report generation and summarization, AI is now deeply involved in many areas. Today's breadth of AI applications is clearly distinct from what was seen before generative AI gained widespread recognition. Representative generative AI services include DALL-E 3 (OpenAI, California, USA) and Stable Diffusion (Stability AI, London, England, UK) for image generation, ChatGPT (OpenAI, California, USA), and Gemini (Google, California, USA) for text generation. The rise of generative AI has been influenced by advances in deep learning models and the scaling up of data, models, and computational resources based on the scaling laws. Moreover, the emergence of foundation models, which are trained on large-scale datasets and possess general-purpose knowledge applicable to various downstream tasks, is creating a new paradigm in AI development.
